                              <span />{task.title}
                            </button>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })() : (
              <>
                <div className="df-date-title">{displayDateTitle(timelineDate)}</div>
                <div className="df-timeline-allday">
                  <span className="df-timeline-allday-label">闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佸壊鍋侀崕杈╃矆婢跺备鍋撻崗澶婁壕闂佸憡娲﹂崐?/span>
                  <div data-all-day-date={timelineDate} className={`df-timeline-allday-content ${drag?.targetAllDay && drag.targetDate === timelineDate ? "drop-active" : ""}`} onDragOver={(event) => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    setDrag((current) => current ? { ...current, outsideTimeline: false, targetDate: timelineDate, targetAllDay: true } : current);
                  }} onDrop={(event) => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    const taskId = event.dataTransfer.getData("taskId") || drag?.taskId;
                    if (taskId) scheduleAllDay(taskId, timelineDate);
                  }}>
                    {tasks.filter((task) => task.scheduledDate === timelineDate && !task.scheduledStart && !task.completed).map((task) => (
                      <AllDayBlock key={task.id} task={task} projectName={projectName(task)} projects={projects} onEdit={() => openTaskEdit(task)} onToggleDone={() => updateTask(task.id, { completed: !task.completed })} onProjectChange={(projectId) => updateTask(task.id, { projectId: projectId || undefined })} onProjectColorChange={(projectId, color) => updateProject(projectId, { color })} onCreateProject={(title) => createProjectForTask(task.id, title)} onDragStart={(event) => {
                        event.dataTransfer.setData("taskId", task.id);
                        setDrag({ taskId: task.id, kind: "candidate", duration: taskDuration(task) });
                      }} onDragEnd={() => setDrag(null)} />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="df-timeline-scroll" ref={timelineRef} onDragOver={(event) => {
                  event.preventDefault();
                  setDrag((current) => current ? { ...current, outsideTimeline: false, targetDate: timelineDate, targetAllDay: false } : current);
                  setHoverSlot(slotFromPointer(event.clientY));
                }} onDrop={(event) => {
                  event.preventDefault();
                  const taskId = event.dataTransfer.getData("taskId") || drag?.taskId;
                  if (taskId) scheduleTask(taskId, hoverSlot || slotFromPointer(event.clientY));
                }} onDragLeave={() => setHoverSlot("")}>
                  <div ref={timelineCanvasRef} data-date={timelineDate} className="df-timeline-canvas" style={{ height: `${((TIMELINE_END - TIMELINE_START) * 60 / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT}px` }} onClick={(event) => {
                    if (drag || resizePreview) return;
                    if ((event.target as HTMLElement).closest(".df-time-block,.df-suggestion,.df-drop-preview,.df-quick-schedule")) return;
                    if (quickSchedule) {
                      setQuickSchedule(null);
                      return;
                    }
                    openQuickSchedule(event.clientY);
                  }}>
                    {Array.from({ length: ((TIMELINE_END - TIMELINE_START) * 60 / SLOT_MINUTES) + 1 }).map((_, index) => {
                      const minutes = TIMELINE_START * 60 + index * SLOT_MINUTES;
                      const isHour = minutes % 60 === 0;
                      const isMajor = minutes % (6 * 60) === 0 && minutes < TIMELINE_END * 60;
                      return <div className={`df-slot ${isHour ? "hour" : "quarter"} ${isMajor ? "major" : ""}`} style={{ top: `${index * SLOT_HEIGHT}px` }} key={minutes}><span>{isHour ? hourLabel(minutes) : ""}</span></div>;
                    })}
                    {isViewingToday && <NowLine />}
                    {quickSchedule && <QuickScheduleInput draft={quickSchedule} projects={projects} onChange={setQuickSchedule} onSave={saveQuickSchedule} onCancel={() => setQuickSchedule(null)} onCreateProject={quickCreateProject} />}
                    {scheduledTasks.length === 0 && suggestions.length === 0 && !drag && <div className="df-timeline-empty"><div className="blob-accent" />闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娴ｈ櫣绀婂┑鐘叉搐缂佲晠姊洪鈧粔鐢稿磻鐎ｎ喗鐓涢柛鎰╁妿婢ф洜绱掗悩宕囧弨闁哄本娲濈粻娑氣偓锝庝簴閸嬫捇寮撮悩鐢电劶婵犮垼鍩栭崝鏍偂閸愵喗鐓忓璺虹墕閸旀岸鏌￠崱娑楁喚闁哄矉缍侀獮姗€宕樺ù瀣壕闁割煈鍣崵鏇㈡煕椤愶絾绀€闁绘挻鐩弻娑氫沪閸撗呯厑闂侀潻绲奸崡鍐差潖濞差亝顥堥柍鍝勫暟琚︽俊鐐€戦崕鏌ュ箲閸ヮ剙鏄ラ柍褜鍓氶妵鍕箳閹存績鍋撹ぐ鎺戝惞闁逞屽墮椤啴濡惰箛娑欘€嶆繝銏㈡嚀濡繂顕ｉ锔绘晪闁逞屽墴閻涱喖螣閸忕厧鐝伴梺鑲┾拡閸撴稑顭囨径鎰拻濞达絿顭堥ˉ蹇涙煕鐎ｎ亝鍣归悡銈嗘叏濡炶浜鹃梺?/div>}
                    {hoverSlot && drag && !drag.outsideTimeline && <PreviewBlock task={tasks.find((task) => task.id === drag.taskId)} startTime={hoverSlot} duration={drag.duration} draggingBlock={drag.kind === "block"} conflict={hasScheduleConflict(hoverSlot, addMinutes(hoverSlot, drag.duration), drag.taskId)} />}
                    {suggestions.filter((item) => !item.ignored).map((suggestion) => <SuggestionBlock key={suggestion.id} suggestion={suggestion} task={tasks.find((task) => task.id === suggestion.taskId)} conflict={suggestionConflict(suggestion)} onApply={() => applySuggestion(suggestion.id)} onIgnore={() => setSuggestions((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === suggestion.id ? { ...item, ignored: true } : item))} />)}
                    {scheduledTasks.filter((task) => !(drag?.kind === "block" && drag.taskId === task.id)).map((task) => (
                      <TimeBlock key={task.id} task={task} preview={resizePreview?.taskId === task.id ? resizePreview : null} projectName={projectName(task)} projects={projects} hovered={hoveredBlock === task.id || resizePreview?.taskId === task.id} onHover={setHoveredBlock} onEdit={() => {
                        if (!suppressBlockClickRef.current) openTaskEdit(task);
                      }} onToggleDone={() => updateTask(task.id, { completed: !task.completed })} onProjectChange={(projectId) => updateTask(task.id, { projectId: projectId || undefined })} onProjectColorChange={(projectId, color) => updateProject(projectId, { color })} onCreateProject={(title) => {
                        createProjectForTask(task.id, title);
                      }} onDragStart={(event) => beginBlockDrag(event, task)} onResizeStart={(event, edge) => beginBlockResize(event, task, edge)} />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </section>
        </main>
      ) : (
        <PlanningView data={data} projects={projects} tasks={tasks} collapsed={collapsedBranches} setCollapsed={setCollapsedBranches} pickMode={planningPickMode} picks={planningPicks} onExitPickMode={() => setPlanningPickMode(false)} onAddPick={addPlanningPick} onUpdatePick={updatePlanningPick} onRemovePick={removePlanningPick} onClearPicks={clearPlanningPicks} onApplyPicks={applyPlanningPicks} onProjectEdit={openProjectEdit} onTaskEdit={openTaskEdit} onTaskUpdate={updateTask} onTaskCreate={createTaskInProject} onTaskDelete={(taskId) => {
          void saveData({ ...data, tasks: data.tasks.filter((task) => task.id !== taskId) });
        }} />
      )}

      <button className="df-add-fab df-icon-action i-plus" data-tip="濠电姷鏁告慨鐑藉极閹间礁纾块柟瀵稿Х缁€濠囨煃瑜滈崜姘跺Φ閸曨垰鍗抽柛鈩冾殔椤忣亪鏌? aria-label="濠电姷鏁告慨鐑藉极閹间礁纾块柟瀵稿Х缁€濠囨煃瑜滈崜姘跺Φ閸曨垰鍗抽柛鈩冾殔椤忣亪鏌? onClick={() => openAdd("task")} />
      <button className="df-ai-fab df-icon-action i-ai" data-tip="闂?Navo" aria-label="settings" onClick={() => setAiOpen((open) => !open)} />

      {drawerOpen && <div className="df-drawer-backdrop" onMouseDown={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} />}
      {drawerOpen && <EditDrawer type={addType} setType={(type) => { setAddType(type); if (!editingId) setForm(defaultForm(type)); }} form={form} setForm={setForm} projects={projects} editing={Boolean(editingId)} task={tasks.find((task) => task.id === editingId)} today={today} advancedOpen={advancedOpen} setAdvancedOpen={(open) => { setAdvancedOpen(open); void saveSettings({ addAdvancedOpen: open }); }} onClose={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} onSave={saveForm} onDelete={deleteEditingItem} onCopy={copyEditingTask} onTaskUpdate={updateTask} onProjectColorChange={(projectId, color) => updateProject(projectId, { color })} onToggleDone={() => updateTask(editingId, { completed: !tasks.find((task) => task.id === editingId)?.completed })} onNextAction={() => void generateNextAction()} onCreateProject={quickCreateProject} />}
      {aiOpen && <AiPanel input={aiInput} setInput={setAiInput} reply={aiReply} busy={aiBusy} onSend={() => void sendAi()} onClose={() => setAiOpen(false)} />}
      {utilityPanel && settings && <UtilityPanel kind={utilityPanel} settings={settings} authEmail={authState?.user?.email || ""} onClose={() => setUtilityPanel(null)} onSave={(patch) => void saveSettings(patch)} onOpenAbout={() => setUtilityPanel("about")} onSignOut={authState?.mode === "cloud" && authState.user ? () => void handleSignOut() : undefined} />}
      {drag?.kind === "block" && drag.outsideTimeline && drag.pointer && <FloatingUnschedulePreview task={tasks.find((task) => task.id === drag.taskId)} pointer={drag.pointer} />}
      {toast && <div className="df-toast">{toast}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function FloatingUnschedulePreview({ task, pointer }: { task?: Task; pointer: { x: number; y: number } }) {
  if (!task) return null;
  return <div className="df-floating-unschedule" style={{ left: pointer.x + 14, top: pointer.y + 14 }}><strong>{task.title}</strong><span>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曚綅閸ャ劌顕遍柡澶嬪灩閿涚喖鎮楃憴鍕婵炶绠撻敐鐐哄即閵忥紕鍘甸柡澶婄墕婢т粙鎮炬潏鈺冪＝濞达絽寮跺▍濠囨煛瀹€鈧崰鏍€佸☉銏犲耿婵°倐鍋撴い蹇ｅ亝閵囧嫰鏁傞崫鍕缂備胶绮粙鎾诲焵椤掑倹鏆╂い顓炵墕閻☆參姊绘担鐟邦嚋婵炴彃绻樺畷鏇熸綇椤愩倓缃曢梻鍌欑閸熷潡骞栭锕€绠犻幖杈剧到椤ユ岸鎮峰▎蹇擃伀缂佺娀绠栭弻娑㈠焺閸忊晜鍨规禍鎼佸Ω閳哄倻鍘?/span></div>;
}

function QuickScheduleInput({ draft, projects, onChange, onSave, onCancel, onCreateProject }: { draft: NonNullable<QuickSchedule>; projects: Project[]; onChange: (draft: NonNullable<QuickSchedule>) => void; onSave: () => void; onCancel: () => void; onCreateProject: (title: string) => string }) {
  const [newProjectTitle, setNewProjectTitle] = useState("");
  const showProjects = /#\S*$/.test(draft.title);
  const hashDraft = draft.title.match(/#(\S*)$/)?.[1] || "";
  const top = ((timeToMinutes(draft.startTime) - TIMELINE_START * 60) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT;
  function chooseProject(project: Project | null) {
    const base = draft.title.replace(/#\S*$/, "").trimEnd();
    onChange({ ...draft, title: `${base}${base ? " " : ""}#${project?.title || "Inbox"}`, projectId: project?.id || "" });
  }
  function createProject() {
    const title = (newProjectTitle || hashDraft).trim();
    if (!title) return;
    const id = onCreateProject(title);
    if (!id) return;
    const base = draft.title.replace(/#\S*$/, "").trimEnd();
    onChange({ ...draft, title: `${base}${base ? " " : ""}#${title}`, projectId: id });
    setNewProjectTitle("");
  }
  return (
    <div className="df-quick-schedule" style={{ top }} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
      {showProjects && <div className="df-project-suggest">
        <button onClick={() => chooseProject(null)}>#闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
        {projects.map((project) => <button key={project.id} onClick={() => chooseProject(project)}>#{project.title}</button>)}
        <div className="df-project-create-line compact">
          <input value={newProjectTitle} placeholder={hashDraft || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閹达絾顥夌痪顓涘亾闂備浇顫夐崕鐓幬涢崟顔剧闂佽楠哥粻宥夊磿闁单鍥ㄥ閺夋垹锛熼柟鍏兼儗濞兼寧绂嶅鍫熺厸闁稿本姘ㄦ禒銏ゆ煙椤旇棄鐏撮柡?} onChange={(event) => setNewProjectTitle(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === "Enter") {
              event.preventDefault();
              createProject();
            }
          }} />
          <button onClick={createProject}>闂?/button>
        </div>
      </div>}
      <input autoFocus value={draft.title} placeholder="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閻旂厧浜伴柣鏂挎娣囧﹪顢涘┑鍡曟睏缂佹儳褰炵划娆撳蓟閻旂厧绠氱憸蹇旂椤忓牊鐓曢悗锝庡亝瀹曞矂鏌熼鎯у幋闁哄苯娲弫鍌炲矗椤愬骸娲﹂悡鐔兼煏韫囧﹥娅呴柣蹇ョ節閺岀喎顫㈢仦钘夋優缂備緡鍠栭澶愮嵁閹烘绫嶉柍褜鍓涢埀顒佺瀹€鎼佸蓟閻旇櫣纾兼俊顖濇〃閸掑﹤鈹?#婵犵數濮烽。顔炬閺囥垹纾婚柟杈剧畱绾惧綊鏌￠崶鈺佸壋闁兼澘娼￠弻娑樜旈崘褏闂? onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...draft, title: event.target.value })} onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if (event.key === "Enter") onSave();
        if (event.key === "Escape") onCancel();
      }} />
      <button className="df-quick-confirm" onClick={onSave}>闂?/button>
    </div>
  );
}

function TaskCard({ task, projects, focusDate, projectName, onQuickDuration, onProjectChange, onReturnPlanning, onSaveNote, onDelete, onToggleDone, onClick, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: { task: Task; projects: Project[]; focusDate: string; projectName: string; onQuickDuration: (minutes: number) => void; onProjectChange: (projectId: string) => void; onReturnPlanning: () => void; onSaveNote: (note: string) => void; onDelete: () => void; onToggleDone: () => void; onClick: () => void; onDragStart: (event: React.DragEvent) => void; onDragEnd: () => void }) {
  const [quickOpen, setQuickOpen] = useState<"duration" | "info" | "note" | "project" | null>(null);
  const [noteDraft, setNoteDraft] = useState(task.notes || "");
  const overdue = task.dueDate < focusDate ? dateDiff(task.dueDate, focusDate) : 0;
  const status = overdue > 0 ? `闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪孩顐介柨鐔哄Т绾惧鏌涢埄鍐槈缁炬儳顭烽弻鐔煎礈瑜滈崝婊堟煠?${overdue} 婵犵數濮烽弫鍛婃叏娴兼潙鍨傞柤娴嬫櫇缁€鍕归崗鍏肩稇缂佲偓?: task.plannedForDate === focusDate ? (focusDate === todayIso() ? "婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繘鏌熺紒銏犳灍闁哄懏绻堥弻鏇熷緞閸繂澹? : "闂傚倷娴囧畷鐢稿窗閹邦喖鍨濋幖娣灪濞呯姵淇婇妶鍛櫣缂佺姳鍗抽幃褰掑炊瑜嶅Λ?) : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ら悧鍡欑矆閸喓绠鹃柛鈩兩戠亸顓犵磼?;
  const stop = (event: React.MouseEvent) => event.stopPropagation();
  return (
    <article className={`df-task-card ${overdue > 0 ? "overdue" : ""}`} draggable onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragEnd={onDragEnd} onClick={onClick}>
      <div className="df-card-strip" style={{ background: categories[task.category].color }} />
      <div className="df-candidate-row">
        <button className="df-candidate-check" title={task.completed ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢妶鍥╃厠闂佸搫顦伴崵姘洪鍕幐闂佸憡绮堥悞锕傚疾閳哄懏鈷戦柟鑲╁仜閸旀挳鏌涢幘瀵告噮闁逞屽墯閼归箖藝娴兼潙桅闁告洦鍠氶悿鈧梺鍦亾閸撴碍瀵奸埀顒勬⒒娴ｅ憡鎯堟俊顐ｇ懃閻ｆ繈骞栨担姝屾憰? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢妶鍥╃厠闂佸搫顦伴崵姘洪鍕幐闂佸憡绮堥悞锕傚疾閳哄懏鈷戦柟鑲╁仜閸旀鏌￠崒娆戠獢鐎规洘鍨块獮妯肩磼濡桨鐢婚梻浣虹帛椤ㄥ懘鎮ф繝鍥х闂侇剙绉甸埛?} onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          onToggleDone();
        }}>{task.completed ? "闂? : ""}</button>
        <strong className="df-candidate-title">{task.title}</strong>
        <button className={`df-duration-pill ${quickOpen === "note" || quickOpen === "project" ? "project-mode" : ""}`} title={quickOpen === "note" || quickOpen === "project" ? "缂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪海鐭嗗〒姘ｅ亾閽樻繈鏌熼崜浣烘憘闁轰礁顑囬幉鍛婃償閵娿儳鐤勯梺闈浥堥弲娑㈡偂濞戙垺鐓曟繛鎴濆船楠炴ɑ銇勯敂璇蹭簼缂佺粯鐩鍫曞箣濠靛洤鈧垶姊烘导娆戠У濞存粠浜畷娲焵椤掍降浜滈柟鍝勬娴滃墽绱撴担鍓叉Ц缂傚秴锕悰? : "婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磿閹寸姴绶ら柦妯侯棦瑜版帒纾奸柣鎰皺閻涖儱鈹戞幊閸婃洟骞婃惔銊ュ嚑濞达絿纭堕弨浠嬫煕鐏炲墽鐭ら柣鎺戝⒔缁辨帡顢曢姀鈶垦囨煛鐏炲墽娲存い銏℃礋閺佹劙宕卞▎妯恍炲┑?} onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          if (quickOpen === "note" || quickOpen === "project") {
            setQuickOpen(quickOpen === "project" ? "note" : "project");
          } else {
            setQuickOpen(quickOpen === "duration" ? null : "duration");
          }
        }}>{quickOpen === "note" || quickOpen === "project" ? "#" : formatDuration(task.estimatedHours || 0.5)}</button>
        <button className={`df-icon-button ${quickOpen === "info" ? "icon-collapse" : "icon-info"}`} title={quickOpen === "info" ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒€违闁逞屽墴閺屾稓鈧綆鍋呭畷宀勬煛瀹€鈧崰鏍€佸☉姗嗙叆闁告劗鍋撳В澶愭煟? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢埛姘そ閺佸啴鍩€椤掑嫧鈧箓宕惰閺嬪酣鏌熼幆褏孝婵炲樊鍙冮悰顕€骞囬鐔峰妳闂佹寧绻傞崐褰掑极閻㈠憡鈷掑ù锝堫潐閻忛亶鏌￠崨顔炬创鐎规洦鍨堕崺锟犲礃閳哄﹥缍?} onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          setQuickOpen(quickOpen === "info" ? null : "info");
        }} />
        <button className="df-icon-button icon-note" title="闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥敋瑜忛幑銏ゅ箳濡も偓绾惧鏌ｉ幇顖ｆ⒖婵炲樊浜滈崘鈧銈嗗姧缂嶅棗顭囬悢鍏尖拺闁荤喖鍋婇崵鐔兼煕鐎ｎ偆銆掓繛鍡愬灲閹瑩鎮滃Ο鐓庡箞闂備線娼ч¨鈧紒鑼跺Г娣囧﹥绂掔€ｎ偆鍘? onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          setQuickOpen(quickOpen === "note" ? null : "note");
        }} />
      </div>
      {quickOpen === "duration" && (
        <div className="df-card-popover duration-list" onClick={stop}>
          {DURATION_OPTIONS.map((minutes) => <button key={minutes} className={Math.round((task.estimatedHours || 0.5) * 60) === minutes ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => {
            onQuickDuration(minutes);
            setQuickOpen(null);
          }}>{formatMinutes(minutes)}</button>)}
        </div>
      )}
      {quickOpen === "info" && (
        <div className="df-card-popover info" onClick={stop}>
          <span>{status}</span>
          <span>{projectName}</span>
          <span>婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻閻愬樊鐒芥繛鍡樻尭鐟欙箓鎮楅敐搴℃灍闁哄拋浜缁樻媴閸涘﹤鏆堥柦鍐含缁辨帞鈧綆鍋呭畷宀勬煙椤栨浜?{priorityLabel[task.priority]}</span>
          <span>闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒婄稏濠㈣埖鍔曠粻姘舵倶閻愭彃鈷旀い鈺佸级缁绘繈妫冨☉鍗炲壘闂?{shortDate(task.dueDate)}</span>
          <button onClick={() => {
            onReturnPlanning();
            setQuickOpen(null);
          }}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒€违闁逞屽墴閺屾稓鈧綆鍋呭畷宀勬煛瀹€鈧崰鏍€佸☉銏犲耿婵°倐鍋撴い蹇ｅ亝閵囧嫰鏁傞崫鍕Е闂佸搫鐭夌紞渚€骞冮姀銈呬紶闁靛绠戝▍鎴︽⒑鐠囨彃顒㈤柛鎴濈秺瀹曟粓鎮㈡搴㈡?/button>
        </div>
      )}
      {quickOpen === "project" && (
        <div className="df-card-popover project-list" onClick={stop}>
          <button onClick={() => {
            onProjectChange("");
            setQuickOpen(null);
          }}># 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
          {projects.map((project) => <button key={project.id} className={String(project.id) === String(task.projectId || "") ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => {
            onProjectChange(project.id);
            setQuickOpen(null);
          }}># {project.title}</button>)}
        </div>
      )}
      {quickOpen === "note" && (
        <div className="df-card-popover note" onClick={stop}>
          <textarea autoFocus rows={4} value={noteDraft} onChange={(event) => setNoteDraft(event.target.value)} placeholder="婵犵數濮烽弫鍛婃叏娴兼潙鍨傞柣鎾崇岸閺嬫牗绻涢幋鐐╂（婵炲樊浜濋弲婵嬫煕鐏炲墽顣查柣? />
          <button onClick={() => {
            onSaveNote(noteDraft);
            setQuickOpen(null);
          }}>婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磿閹寸姴绶ら柦妯侯棦濞差亝鏅滈柣鎰靛墮鎼村﹪姊虹粙璺ㄧ伇闁稿鍋ゅ畷?/button>
        </div>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}

function formatMinutes(minutes: number) {
  const rounded = Math.max(0, Math.round(minutes));
  if (rounded < 60) return `${rounded}m`;
  const hours = Math.floor(rounded / 60);
  const rest = rounded % 60;
  return rest ? `${hours}h${rest}m` : `${hours}h`;
}

function formatDuration(hours: number) {
  return formatMinutes(Math.round(hours * 60));
}

function TimeBlock({ task, preview, projectName, projects, hovered, onHover, onEdit, onToggleDone, onProjectChange, onProjectColorChange, onCreateProject, onDragStart, onResizeStart }: { task: Task; preview: ResizePreview; projectName: string; projects: Project[]; hovered: boolean; onHover: (id: string) => void; onEdit: () => void; onToggleDone: () => void; onProjectChange: (projectId: string) => void; onProjectColorChange: (projectId: string, color: string) => void; onCreateProject: (title: string) => void; onDragStart: (event: React.MouseEvent) => void; onResizeStart: (event: React.MouseEvent, edge: "start" | "end") => void }) {
  const [projectOpen, setProjectOpen] = useState(false);
  const [newProjectTitle, setNewProjectTitle] = useState("");
  const start = preview?.start || task.scheduledStart || "09:00";
  const end = preview?.end || task.scheduledEnd || addMinutes(start, taskDuration(task));
  const top = ((timeToMinutes(start) - TIMELINE_START * 60) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT;
  const height = Math.max(((timeToMinutes(end) - timeToMinutes(start)) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT, 38);
  const next = extractNextAction(task.notes);
  const stripeColor = projects.find((project) => String(project.id) === String(task.projectId || ""))?.color || categories[task.category].color;
  return (
    <div className={`df-time-block priority-${task.priority} ${task.completed ? "completed" : ""} ${preview ? "resizing" : ""} ${projectOpen ? "project-open" : ""}`} style={{ top, height, "--cat": stripeColor } as CSSProperties} onMouseEnter={() => onHover(task.id)} onMouseLeave={() => {
      onHover("");
      setProjectOpen(false);
    }} onMouseDown={onDragStart} onClick={onEdit} onDoubleClick={onEdit}>
      <div className="df-category-strip" />
      {hovered && <button className="df-resize-dot top" aria-label="闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥х暦閸偅鍙忛柟缁㈠櫘閺佸嫰鏌涘☉娆愮稇闁汇値鍠楅妵鍕冀椤愵澀绮跺┑顔款潐閻擄繝寮婚妸銉㈡斀闁糕剝锚椤庢盯姊洪幖鐐测偓鏍洪悢鐓庤摕鐎广儱鐗滃銊╂⒑閸涘﹥灏扮€光偓閹间胶宓侀柛顐犲劚鎯熼悷婊冾樀瀹曟垿骞樼紒妯绘珳闁硅偐琛ラ埀顒冨皺閸戣淇婇悙顏冪礀濞达絽鎲℃径鍕煃? onMouseDown={(event) => onResizeStart(event, "start")} />}
      <div className="df-block-title-row">
        <button className="df-block-check" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()} onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          onToggleDone();
        }} aria-label={task.completed ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢妶鍥╃厠闂佸搫顦伴崵姘洪鍕幐闂佸憡绮堥悞锕傚疾閳哄懏鈷戦柟鑲╁仜閸旀挳鏌涢幘瀵告噮闁逞屽墯閼归箖藝娴兼潙桅闁告洦鍠氶悿鈧梺鍦亾閸撴碍瀵奸埀顒勬⒒娴ｅ憡鎯堟俊顐ｇ懃閻ｆ繈骞栨担姝屾憰? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢妶鍥╃厠闂佸搫顦伴崵姘洪鍕幐闂佸憡绮堥悞锕傚疾閳哄懏鈷戦柟鑲╁仜閸旀鏌￠崒娆戠獢鐎规洘鍨块獮妯肩磼濡桨鐢婚梻浣虹帛椤ㄥ懘鎮ф繝鍥х闂侇剙绉甸埛?}>{task.completed ? "闂? : ""}</button>
        <strong>{task.title}</strong>
        {hovered && <span className="df-block-project-wrap" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
          <button className="df-block-project" onClick={() => setProjectOpen((open) => !open)}># {projectName}</button>
          {projectOpen && <div className="df-project-popover">
            <button onClick={() => { onProjectChange(""); setProjectOpen(false); }}># 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
            {projects.map((project) => <ProjectChoice key={project.id} project={project} onChoose={() => { onProjectChange(project.id); setProjectOpen(false); }} onColorChange={(color) => onProjectColorChange(project.id, color)} />)}
            <div className="df-project-create-line"><input value={newProjectTitle} placeholder="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閹达絾顥夌痪顓涘亾闂備浇顫夐崕鐓幬涢崟顔剧闂佽楠哥粻宥夊磿闁单鍥ㄥ閺夋垹锛熼柟鍏兼儗濞兼寧绂嶅鍫熺厸闁稿本姘ㄦ禒銏ゆ煙椤旇棄鐏撮柡? onChange={(event) => setNewProjectTitle(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter") { event.preventDefault(); onCreateProject(newProjectTitle); setNewProjectTitle(""); setProjectOpen(false); } }} /><button onClick={() => { onCreateProject(newProjectTitle); setNewProjectTitle(""); setProjectOpen(false); }}>闂?/button></div>
          </div>}
        </span>}
      </div>
      {next && <span className="df-next">婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻閻愬搫鍨傞柛顐ｆ礀缁犱即鏌熼梻瀵歌窗闁轰礁瀚伴弻娑㈠即閵娿儱绠婚梺鍛婎殕瀹€鎼佸蓟閳╁啫绶為悗锝庝簽閸旂鈹戦悙鐑橈紵闁告濞婂濠氬即閻旈绐炲┑鈽嗗灥濞咃絾绂掗婊呯＝濞达絿鎳撴慨鍫ユ煃瀹勬壆宕竐xt}</span>}
      {hovered && <button className="df-resize-dot bottom" aria-label="闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥х暦閸偅鍙忛柟缁㈠櫘閺佸嫰鏌涘☉娆愮稇闁汇値鍠楅妵鍕冀椤愵澀绮跺┑顔款潐閻擄繝寮婚妸銉㈡斀闁糕剝锚缁愭稓绱掗悙顒€鍔ら柕鍫熸倐瀵鎮㈤悡搴ｇ暰閻熸粌绻橀獮妤呮偐缂佹ê鈧爼鐓崶銊﹀鞍妞ゃ儯鍨介弻鈥崇暆鐎ｎ剛鐦堥悗瑙勬礃閿曘垺淇婂宀婃Щ闂佺粯鎸鹃崰搴ㄥ煘? onMouseDown={(event) => onResizeStart(event, "end")} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PreviewBlock({ task, startTime, duration, draggingBlock, conflict }: { task?: Task; startTime: string; duration: number; draggingBlock?: boolean; conflict?: boolean }) {
  if (!task) return null;
  const top = ((timeToMinutes(startTime) - TIMELINE_START * 60) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT;
  const height = Math.max((duration / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT, 38);
  return <div className={`df-drop-preview ${draggingBlock ? "moving-block" : ""} ${conflict ? "conflict" : ""}`} style={{ top, height }}><strong>{task.title}</strong>{!draggingBlock && <span>{conflict ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鏁撻悩鑼槷濠碘槅鍨跺Λ鍧楁倿婵犲啰绠鹃柛鈩兩戠亸浼存煕? : startTime} 闂?{Math.round(duration)}min</span>}</div>;
}

function SuggestionBlock({ suggestion, task, conflict, onApply, onIgnore }: { suggestion: ScheduleSuggestion; task?: Task; conflict: boolean; onApply: () => void; onIgnore: () => void }) {
  if (!task) return null;
  const top = ((timeToMinutes(suggestion.startTime) - TIMELINE_START * 60) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT;
  const height = Math.max(((timeToMinutes(suggestion.endTime) - timeToMinutes(suggestion.startTime)) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT, 48);
  return <div className={`df-suggestion ${conflict ? "conflict" : ""}`} style={{ top, height }}>
    <button className="df-suggestion-action apply" disabled={conflict} aria-label="闂傚倸鍊风粈浣革耿闁秴鍌ㄧ憸鏃堝箖濞差亜惟闁靛鍠楃紞搴㈢節閻㈤潧校闁煎綊绠栧鍛婄瑹閳ь剟寮婚弴銏犻唶婵犻潧娲ら娑㈡⒑閹肩偛鈧洟鎮ч崱姘变簷闂備焦瀵у濠氭偤閺冨牆纾块柕澶嗘櫆閻? onClick={onApply}>闂?/button>
    <button className="df-suggestion-action ignore" aria-label="婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻閻愬搫鍨傞柛顐ｆ礀缁犱即鏌涘┑鍕姢闁活厽鎸鹃幉鎼佹偋閸繄鐟ㄩ梻鍌氬亞閸ㄤ即婀侀梺缁樻尭濞寸兘骞楅悩缁樼厱閻庯絻鍔屾慨鍌炴煛鐏炵偓绀冪紒缁樼洴瀹曞綊顢欓悡骞垹绱撻崒娆戝妽闁哥偠宕电划濠氬箻鐠囪尙鐣? onClick={onIgnore}>闂?/button>
    <span>AI 闂傚倷娴囬褍霉閻戣棄纾婚柨婵嗩槸绾捐绻濋棃娑氬ⅱ缁炬崘濮ら幈銊ヮ潨閳ь剟路閸岀偛鏋?{conflict && "闂?闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鏁撻悩鑼槷濠碘槅鍨跺Λ鍧楁倿婵犲啰绠鹃柛鈩兩戠亸浼存煕?}</span><strong>{task.title}</strong><small>{suggestion.reason}</small>{suggestion.nextAction && <em>婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻閻愬搫鍨傞柛顐ｆ礀缁犱即鏌熼梻瀵歌窗闁轰礁瀚伴弻娑㈠即閵娿儱绠婚梺鍛婎殕瀹€鎼佸蓟閳╁啫绶為悗锝庝簽閸旂鈹戦悙鐑橈紵闁告濞婂濠氬即閻旈绐炲┑鈽嗗灥濞咃絾绂掗婊呯＝濞达絿鎳撴慨鍫ユ煃瀹勬壆宓剈ggestion.nextAction}</em>}
  </div>;
}

function ProjectColorPicker({ value, onChange, compact = false, presets = PROJECT_COLOR_PRESETS }: { value: string; onChange: (color: string) => void; compact?: boolean; presets?: string[] }) {
  const isCustom = !presets.includes(value);
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(value);
  const isLight = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b) > 195;
  return (
    <div className={`df-project-color-picker ${compact ? "compact" : ""} ${isCustom ? "custom-active" : ""} ${isLight ? "color-light" : ""}`}>
      {presets.map((color) => {
        const { r: pr, g: pg, b: pb } = hexToRgb(color);
        const pLight = (0.299 * pr + 0.587 * pg + 0.114 * pb) > 195;
        return <button key={color} type="button" className={`${value === color ? "active" : ""} ${pLight ? "color-light" : ""}`} style={{ "--project-color": color } as CSSProperties} aria-label={color} onClick={() => onChange(color)} />;
      })}
      <label className="df-project-color-custom" style={{ "--project-color": value } as CSSProperties} title="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洖鍌ㄧ憸鏃堝Υ閸愨晜鍎熼柕蹇嬪焺濞茬鈹戦悩璇у伐闁绘锕畷鎴﹀煛閸涱喚鍘介梺閫涘嵆濞佳勬櫠娴煎瓨鐓?beta)">
        <input type="color" value={value} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)} />
        <span />
      </label>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProjectChoice({ project, onChoose, onColorChange }: { project: Project; onChoose: () => void; onColorChange: (color: string) => void }) {
  const [colorOpen, setColorOpen] = useState(false);
  const color = project.color || categories[project.category].color;
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(color);
  const colorLight = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b) > 195;
  return (
    <div className="df-project-choice">
      <button type="button" onClick={onChoose}># {project.title}</button>
      <span className="df-project-color-menu" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <button type="button" className="df-project-color-dot-button" aria-label={`${project.title} 婵犵數濮烽。钘壩ｉ崨鏉戠；闁糕剝蓱濞呯姵淇婇妶鍛殭闁搞劍绻堥悡顐﹀炊閵婏腹鎷婚梺缁樼箚濞夋盯鍩為幋锕€纾兼繝濠傚閸?onClick={() => setColorOpen((open) => !open)}><span className={`df-project-color-dot ${colorLight ? "color-light" : ""}`} style={{ "--project-color": color } as CSSProperties} /></button>
        {colorOpen && <ProjectColorPicker value={color} onChange={(nextColor) => { onColorChange(nextColor); setColorOpen(false); }} compact />}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function QuickProjectPicker(props: {
  projects: Project[];
  value: string;
  open: boolean;
  newTitle: string;
  newColor: string;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onChange: (projectId: string) => void;
  onTitleChange: (title: string) => void;
  onColorChange: (color: string) => void;
  onProjectColorChange: (projectId: string, color: string) => void;
  onCreate: () => void;
}) {
  const selected = props.projects.find((project) => String(project.id) === String(props.value));
  const selectedColor = selected?.color || PROJECT_COLOR_PRESETS[0];
  const { r: sr, g: sg, b: sb } = hexToRgb(selectedColor);
  const selectedColorLight = (0.299 * sr + 0.587 * sg + 0.114 * sb) > 195;
  return (
    <div className="df-quick-project-picker" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
      <button type="button" className="df-quick-project-trigger" onClick={() => props.onOpenChange(!props.open)}>
        <span className={`df-project-color-dot ${selectedColorLight ? "color-light" : ""}`} style={{ "--project-color": selectedColor } as CSSProperties} />
        <span>{selected ? `# ${selected.title}` : "#"}</span>
      </button>
      {props.open && <div className="df-project-popover df-quick-project-popover up">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => { props.onChange(""); props.onOpenChange(false); }}># 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
        {props.projects.map((project) => <ProjectChoice key={project.id} project={project} onChoose={() => { props.onChange(project.id); props.onOpenChange(false); }} onColorChange={(color) => props.onProjectColorChange(project.id, color)} />)}
        <div className="df-project-create-line df-project-create-with-color">
          <input value={props.newTitle} placeholder="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閹达絾顥夌痪顓涘亾闂備浇顫夐崕鐓幬涢崟顔剧闂佽楠哥粻宥夊磿闁单鍥ㄥ閺夋垹锛熼柟鍏兼儗濞兼寧绂嶅鍫熺厸闁稿本姘ㄦ禒銏ゆ煙椤旇棄鐏撮柡? onChange={(event) => props.onTitleChange(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter") { event.preventDefault(); props.onCreate(); } }} />
          <ProjectColorPicker value={props.newColor} onChange={props.onColorChange} compact />
          <button type="button" onClick={props.onCreate}>闂?/button>
        </div>
      </div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function AllDayBlock({ task, projectName, projects, onEdit, onToggleDone, onProjectChange, onProjectColorChange, onCreateProject, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: { task: Task; projectName: string; projects: Project[]; onEdit: () => void; onToggleDone: () => void; onProjectChange: (projectId: string) => void; onProjectColorChange: (projectId: string, color: string) => void; onCreateProject: (title: string) => void; onDragStart: (event: React.DragEvent) => void; onDragEnd: () => void }) {
  const [projectOpen, setProjectOpen] = useState(false);
  const [newProjectTitle, setNewProjectTitle] = useState("");
  const stripeColor = projects.find((project) => String(project.id) === String(task.projectId || ""))?.color || categories[task.category].color;
  return (
    <article className={`df-all-day-block ${task.completed ? "completed" : ""} ${projectOpen ? "project-open" : ""}`} draggable style={{ "--cat": stripeColor } as CSSProperties} onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragEnd={onDragEnd} onClick={onEdit} onMouseLeave={() => setProjectOpen(false)}>
      <div className="df-category-strip" />
      <button className="df-block-check" onClick={(event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        onToggleDone();
      }}>{task.completed ? "闂? : ""}</button>
      <strong>{task.title}</strong>
      <span className="df-block-project-wrap" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <button className="df-block-project" onClick={() => setProjectOpen((open) => !open)}># {projectName}</button>
        {projectOpen && <div className="df-project-popover">
          <button onClick={() => { onProjectChange(""); setProjectOpen(false); }}># 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
          {projects.map((project) => <ProjectChoice key={project.id} project={project} onChoose={() => { onProjectChange(project.id); setProjectOpen(false); }} onColorChange={(color) => onProjectColorChange(project.id, color)} />)}
          <div className="df-project-create-line"><input value={newProjectTitle} placeholder="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閹达絾顥夌痪顓涘亾闂備浇顫夐崕鐓幬涢崟顔剧闂佽楠哥粻宥夊磿闁单鍥ㄥ閺夋垹锛熼柟鍏兼儗濞兼寧绂嶅鍫熺厸闁稿本姘ㄦ禒銏ゆ煙椤旇棄鐏撮柡? onChange={(event) => setNewProjectTitle(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter") { event.preventDefault(); onCreateProject(newProjectTitle); setNewProjectTitle(""); setProjectOpen(false); } }} /><button onClick={() => { onCreateProject(newProjectTitle); setNewProjectTitle(""); setProjectOpen(false); }}>闂?/button></div>
        </div>}
      </span>
    </article>
  );
}

function NowLine() {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 60000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const minutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  if (minutes < TIMELINE_START * 60 || minutes > TIMELINE_END * 60) return null;
  const top = ((minutes - TIMELINE_START * 60) / SLOT_MINUTES) * SLOT_HEIGHT;
  return <div className="df-now-line" style={{ top }}><span>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸撲礁鍨濇い鏍ㄧ矊閸ㄦ繄鈧箍鍎遍幏瀣偄閸℃ü绻嗘い鏍ㄧ矊閻ㄦ垿鏌?/span></div>;
}

function EditDrawer(props: {
  type: AddType; setType: (type: AddType) => void; form: FormState; setForm: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<FormState>>; projects: Project[]; editing: boolean; task?: Task; today: string; advancedOpen: boolean; setAdvancedOpen: (open: boolean) => void; onClose: () => void; onSave: () => void; onDelete: () => void; onCopy: () => void; onTaskUpdate: (taskId: string, patch: Partial<Task>) => void; onProjectColorChange: (projectId: string, color: string) => void; onToggleDone: () => void; onNextAction: () => void; onCreateProject: (title: string) => string;
}) {
  const [newProjectTitle, setNewProjectTitle] = useState("");
  const [projectPickerOpen, setProjectPickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [moreOpen, setMoreOpen] = useState(false);
  const f = props.form;
  const set = (key: keyof FormState, value: FormState[keyof FormState]) => props.setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: value }));
  const selectedProjectTitle = props.projects.find((project) => String(project.id) === String(f.projectId))?.title || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?;
  const priorityText = f.priority === "high" ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佲€﹂鍕；闁告洦鍊嬪ú顏勎у璺猴功閻ゅ洤鈹戦濮愪粶闁稿鎸搁埞鎴︽晬閸曞灚顥撶紓浣哄У缁嬫垿鍩ユ径濞㈢喓娑垫搴¤€? : f.priority === "low" ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕闂佺硶鍓濋摂瀣炊椤掍胶鍔堕悗骞垮劚閹虫劙宕㈤幘顔解拺闁告繂瀚埢澶愭煕濡灝浜瑰? : "闂傚倸鍊风粈浣革耿闁秴鍌ㄧ憸鏃堝箖濞差亜惟闁靛瀛╃粙鎺旂箔閻旂厧鐒垫い鎺戝閳ь兛绀侀～婵嬵敆婢跺鍏婇梺鑽ゅ█缂傛艾鈻嶉敐澶嬫櫖?;
  function createAndSelectProject() {
    const id = props.onCreateProject(newProjectTitle);
    if (!id) return;
    set("projectId", id);
    setNewProjectTitle("");
    setProjectPickerOpen(false);
  }
  function setDurationMinutes(minutes: number) {
    const safeMinutes = Math.max(minutes, SLOT_MINUTES);
    set("estimatedHours", safeMinutes / 60);
    if (!props.editing || props.type !== "task" || !props.task) return;
    const patch: Partial<Task> = { estimatedHours: safeMinutes / 60 };
    if (props.task.scheduledStart) patch.scheduledEnd = addMinutes(props.task.scheduledStart, safeMinutes);
    props.onTaskUpdate(props.task.id, patch);
  }
  function addSubtask() {
    if (!props.task) return;
    const title = window.prompt("闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥敋瑜忛埀顒佺▓閺呮繄鍒掑▎鎾崇婵°倐鍋撶紒鈧径鎰厵闁绘垶锕╁▓鏃傜磼閻樺磭鍙€闁哄本娲濈粻娑氣偓锝庝簴閸嬫捇寮撮悩鐢电劶婵犮垼鍩栭崝鏍偂閸愵喗鐓忓璺虹墕婵′粙鏌ｅ┑鎰偗闁哄本绋掔换婵嬪磼濠婃劗绀婄紓?);
    if (!title?.trim()) return;
    props.onTaskUpdate(props.task.id, {
      subtasks: [...(props.task.subtasks || []), { id: uid("subtask"), title: title.trim(), completed: false, done: false, order: Date.now(), createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }]
    });
  }
  function updateSubtask(subtaskId: string, patch: { title?: string; completed?: boolean }) {
    if (!props.task) return;
    props.onTaskUpdate(props.task.id, {
      subtasks: (props.task.subtasks || []).map((subtask) => subtask.id === subtaskId ? { ...subtask, ...patch, done: patch.completed ?? subtask.done } : subtask)
    });
  }
  function scheduleText(task: Task) {
    if (task.scheduledDate && task.scheduledStart && task.scheduledEnd) return `${task.scheduledDate} ${task.scheduledStart} - ${task.scheduledEnd} 闂?${formatDuration(taskDuration(task) / 60)}`;
    if (task.plannedForDate === props.today) return "婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繘鏌熺紒銏犳灍闁哄懏绻堥弻鏇㈠醇濠垫劖笑婵?闂?闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊绘晶娑㈡煕鐎Ｑ勬珚闁哄本娲樼换娑㈡倷椤掍胶褰呯紓鍌欒兌婵灚绻涙繝鍥ц摕闁绘柨鍚嬮崐缁樹繆椤栨繃顏犲ù鐘虫尦閹鎮介棃娑辨毉闂佸搫琚崝鎴濓耿娓氣偓濮婃椽骞愭惔锝囩暤濠碘槅鍋呴悷褎绂嶉幖浣瑰亱闁割偅绮庣粻姘舵⒑缂佹ê濮堢憸鏉垮暣閸┾偓妞ゆ巻鍋撻柣蹇旂箞閿?;
    if (task.dueDate) return `${task.dueDate} 闂?${formatDuration(f.estimatedHours || 0.5)}`;
    return "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊绘晶娑㈡煕鐎Ｑ勬珚闁哄本娲樼换娑㈡倷椤掍胶褰呯紓?;
  }
  if (props.editing && props.type === "task" && props.task) {
    const isCandidate = props.task.plannedForDate === props.today && !props.task.scheduledDate;
    const isScheduled = props.task.scheduledDate === props.today || Boolean(props.task.scheduledDate && props.task.scheduledStart);
    return (
      <aside className="df-drawer df-task-detail">
        <div className="df-drawer-head"><h2>婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繐螖閿濆懎鏋ら柡浣割儑閹插憡鎯旈妸銉х杽闂侀潧艌閺呮粓宕戦崟顖涚厽闁瑰浼濋鍫熷仧濞寸厧鐡ㄩ埛鎴︽煙缁嬪灝顒㈡い銉ユ娣囧﹪顢曢姀鐙€浼冮悗?/h2><div className="df-detail-head-actions"><button className="df-icon-action i-more" data-tip="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢埛姘そ閺佸啴鍩€椤掑嫧鈧箓宕惰閺嬪酣鏌? aria-label="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢埛姘そ閺佸啴鍩€椤掑嫧鈧箓宕惰閺嬪酣鏌? onClick={() => setMoreOpen((open) => !open)} />{moreOpen && <div className="df-detail-more"><button onClick={() => { props.onCopy(); setMoreOpen(false); }}>婵犵數濮烽弫鍛婃叏娴兼潙鍨傞柣鎾崇岸閺嬫牗绻涢幋鐐寸殤闁活厽鎹囬弻鐔虹磼閵忕姵鐏堥梺鍝勫閸庡弶绌辨繝鍥ч柛鏇ㄥ枛閸樷€愁渻閵堝倹娅囬柛蹇旓耿楠炲啳銇愰幒鎴犲€為梺闈涱焾閸庡磭绮婂畡閭︽富?/button><button onClick={() => setProjectPickerOpen(true)}>缂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪海鐭嗗〒姘ｅ亾閽樻繈鏌熼崜浣烘憘闁轰礁顑囬幉鍛婃償閵娿儳鐤勯梺闈浥堥弲娑㈡偂濞戙垺鐓曟繛鎴濆船楠炴ɑ銇勯敂璇蹭簼缂佺粯鐩鍫曞箣濠靛洤鈧垶姊烘导娆戠У濞存粠浜畷娲焵椤掍降浜滈柟鍝勬娴滃墽绱撴担鍓叉Ц缂傚秴锕悰?/button><button className="danger" onClick={props.onDelete}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鏁嶉崟顒佹闂佺粯鍔曢顓犵不妤ｅ啯鐓冪憸婊堝礈濮樿鲸宕叉繛鎴欏灩瀹告繃銇勯幘璺哄壉闁告柨顦辩槐鎾存媴缁嬪簱鍋撻崷顓熸殰闁绘劕鎼粻顖炴煟濡鍤欑紒鐘垫暬閺岀喖鎮滃Ο鑲╃暫闂?/button></div>}<button className="df-icon-action i-close" data-tip="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? aria-label="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? onClick={props.onClose} /></div></div>
        <section className="df-detail-title">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={props.task.completed} onChange={props.onToggleDone} />
          <div>
            <input value={f.title} onChange={(event) => set("title", event.target.value)} />
            <span>{props.task.completed ? "闂傚倷娴囬褍霉閻戣棄绠犻柟鎹愵嚙缁犵喖姊介崶顒€桅闁圭増婢樼粈鍐┿亜閺冨洦顥夊ù鐘靛帶椤啴濡堕崱妤冪懆闁诲孩鍑归崜娑氬垝? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊绘晶娑㈡煕鐎Ｑ勬珕闁靛洤瀚伴獮鎺楀箣濠垫劒鎮ｇ紓?} 闂?{isScheduled ? "婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繘鏌熺紒銏犳灍闁哄懏绻堥弻鏇熷緞閸繂澹斿┑鐐村灟閸ㄥ綊鎮″☉銏＄厱婵炴垵宕獮妯汇亜閹剧偨鍋㈤柟顔煎槻椤劑宕熼銈勭礉闂? : isCandidate ? "婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繘鏌熺紒銏犳灍闁哄懏绻堥弻鏇㈠醇濠垫劖笑婵? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊绘晶娑㈡煕鐎Ｑ勬珚闁哄本娲樼换娑㈡倷椤掍胶褰呯紓?} 闂?{priorityText}</span>
          </div>
        </section>

      {props.type === "task" && <><label>婵犵數濮烽。顔炬閺囥垹纾婚柟杈剧畱绾惧綊鏌￠崶鈺佸壋闁兼澘娼￠弻娑樜旈崘褏闂?div className="df-drawer-project-picker"><button type="button" onClick={() => setProjectPickerOpen((open) => !open)}># {selectedProjectTitle}</button>{projectPickerOpen && <div className="df-drawer-project-list"><button onClick={() => { set("projectId", ""); setProjectPickerOpen(false); }}># 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>{props.projects.map((project) => <ProjectChoice key={project.id} project={project} onChoose={() => { set("projectId", project.id); setProjectPickerOpen(false); }} onColorChange={(color) => props.onProjectColorChange(project.id, color)} />)}<div className="df-project-create-line compact"><input value={newProjectTitle} placeholder="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧壕褰掓煟閹达絾顥夌痪顓涘亾闂備浇顫夐崕鐓幬涢崟顔剧闂佽楠哥粻宥夊磿闁单鍥ㄥ閺夋垹锛熼柟鍏兼儗濞兼寧绂嶅鍫熺厸闁稿本姘ㄦ禒銏ゆ煙椤旇棄鐏撮柡? onChange={(event) => setNewProjectTitle(event.target.value)} onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Enter") { event.preventDefault(); createAndSelectProject(); } }} /><button onClick={createAndSelectProject}>闂?/button></div></div>}</div></label><label>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敃鈧悿顕€鏌曟繛鐐珔缁炬儳娼￠弻鐔虹磼濡椽鍤嬮梺?input type="date" value={f.dueDate} onChange={(event) => set("dueDate", event.target.value)} /></label></>}

      {props.advancedOpen && <div className="df-advanced">{props.type === "task" && <label>婵犵數濮烽。钘壩ｉ崨鏉戠；闁告侗鍙庨悢鍡樹繆椤栨氨姣為柛瀣尭椤繈鎮℃惔锝勬婵＄偑鍊戦崹娲晝閵忋倗宓侀柡宥冨妽婵挳鏌ｉ悢纰辨殥缂傚倹鎹囧缁樼瑹閳ь剙顭囪閳ワ箓顢橀姀鈾€鎸冮梺鍛婃处閸?select value={Math.max(Math.round((f.estimatedHours || 0.25) * 60), SLOT_MINUTES)} onChange={(event) => setDurationMinutes(Number(event.target.value))}>{DURATION_OPTIONS.map((minutes) => <option key={minutes} value={minutes}>{formatMinutes(minutes)}</option>)}</select></label>}<label>婵犵數濮烽弫鍛婃叏娴兼潙鍨傞柣鎾崇岸閺嬫牗绻涢幋鐐╂（婵炲樊浜濋弲婵嬫煕鐏炲墽顣查柣?textarea rows={6} value={f.details} onChange={(event) => set("details", event.target.value)} /></label></div>}

    </aside>

function AiPanel({ input, setInput, reply, busy, onSend, onClose }: { input: string; setInput: (v: string) => void; reply: string; busy: boolean; onSend: () => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  return <aside className="df-ai-panel"><div><strong>NavoPath AI</strong><button className="df-icon-action i-close" data-tip="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? aria-label="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? onClick={onClose} /></div><textarea value={input} onChange={(event) => setInput(event.target.value)} /><button className="df-icon-action i-send" data-tip={busy ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒夋晪闁哄稁鍘肩粈鍫熸叏濡潡鍝虹€规洘鐓￠弻娑㈠焺閸愵亖妲堥梺缁樺笒閻忔岸濡甸崟顖氱闁糕剝銇炴竟鏇㈡⒒娴ｅ憡鎲搁柛鐔锋健瀵煡鎮╃拠鑼枃? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鏁撻悩鍐蹭画闂佹寧娲栭崐鎼佸垂閸岀偞鐓曠憸搴ㄣ€冮崨瀛樺€?} aria-label={busy ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒夋晪闁哄稁鍘肩粈鍫熸叏濡潡鍝虹€规洘鐓￠弻娑㈠焺閸愵亖妲堥梺缁樺笒閻忔岸濡甸崟顖氱闁糕剝銇炴竟鏇㈡⒒娴ｅ憡鎲搁柛鐔锋健瀵煡鎮╃拠鑼枃? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鏁撻悩鍐蹭画闂佹寧娲栭崐鎼佸垂閸岀偞鐓曠憸搴ㄣ€冮崨瀛樺€?} onClick={onSend} disabled={busy || !input.trim()} />{reply && <pre>{reply}</pre>}</aside>;
}

function UtilityPanel({ kind, settings, authEmail, onClose, onSave, onOpenAbout, onSignOut }: { kind: "settings" | "about"; settings: Settings; authEmail: string; onClose: () => void; onSave: (patch: Partial<Settings>) => void; onOpenAbout: () => void; onSignOut?: () => void }) {
  if (kind === "settings") {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="df-utility-backdrop" onMouseDown={onClose} />
        <aside className="df-utility-panel">
          <div className="df-utility-head">
            <h2>闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥х暦閸偅鍙忕€规洖娲ㄩ惌鍡椕归敐鍫綈婵炲懐濮撮湁闁绘ê妯婇崕鎰版煕?/h2>
            <button className="df-icon-action i-close" aria-label="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? onClick={onClose} />
          </div>
          <div className="df-utility-body">
            <section className="df-settings-profile">
              <div className="df-settings-profile-avatar">
                <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 21c0-4.4 3.6-8 8-8s8 3.6 8 8"/></svg>
              </div>
              <div>
                <strong>{settings.displayName || authEmail || "NavoPath User"}</strong>
                {authEmail && <span>{authEmail}</span>}
                <small>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佸綊鍋婇崰姘舵儗閹剧粯鐓熼柟杈剧稻椤ュ鏌涢妸銉モ偓鍧楀蓟濞戞鏆嗛柍褜鍓熷畷鎴︽倷閸濆嫮锛?/small>
              </div>
            </section>

              <li>婵犵數濮烽。顔炬閺囥垹纾婚柟杈剧畱绾惧綊鏌￠崶鈺佸壋闁兼澘娼￠弻娑樜旈崘褏闂┑鐐叉▕娴滄繈宕戠€ｎ喗鐓曟い鎰靛亜婢т即鏌ｉ鐕佹當闁宠鍨块幃鈺冩嫚瑜嶆导鎰攽閳藉棗浜濇い銊ワ龚濡垽妫呴銏″缂佸甯￠幃锟犳偐鐠囪尙楠囬梺鍓插亝缁嬫垶淇婃禒瀣厽闁规儳宕崝姘舵煃鐟欏嫬鐏存い銏＄懅閹叉挳宕熼鍌氱秲闂佽瀛╅鏍闯椤曗偓瀹曟娊鏁愰崨顖涙婵犻潧鍊婚…鍫ュ础閹惰姤鐓熼柡鍥╁仜閳ь剙顭峰畷銉ㄣ亹閹烘挴鎷洪梺鍛婄箓鐎氬嘲危濞差亝鐓曢悗锝庡墮閺嬫棃鏌熼獮鍨仼闁宠鍨归埀顒婄秵娴滅偤顢欐径鎰拺闂傚牊绋撶粻鐐烘煕婵犲啯绀堢紒顔肩墛瀵板嫰骞囬娑欏瘱闂備焦鎮堕崕顖炲礉鎼淬劍鍋熼悹楦裤€€閺€浠嬫煟濡搫绾ч柟鍏煎姉缁辨帡鎮▎蹇旀悙缂佺姵濞婇弻鐔煎箚閻楀牜妫勯梺?/li>
              <li>AI 闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶褜娴栭柕濞炬櫆閸婂潡鏌ㄩ弴鐐测偓褰掑磻閿涘嫭鍠愰煫鍥ㄧ☉缁€鍡涙煙閻戞ɑ鐓ｆ繛宀婁邯閺岋綁骞囬棃娑橆潾缂備礁顦悥鐓庮潖濞差亜宸濆┑鐘辫兌缁讳線姊洪崨濠庣劸濠㈢懓鐗撹矾闁告稑鐡ㄩ埛鎺懨归敐鍥у妺闁搞倐鍋撻梻浣规偠閸ㄩ缚銇愰崘顓犱罕婵犵數濮撮敃銈呂涢銏犳辈闁哄绨遍弨浠嬫煟閹邦厽缍戦柣蹇ョ畵閺岀喖宕ㄦ繝鍐ㄢ偓鎰亜閵忥紕澧电€规洜鍠栭、姗€鎮╅幓鎺曅曢梻鍌欑閹碱偊藝娴兼潙鏄ラ柛顐ｇ箥閻掕棄霉閻撳海鎽犻柣鎾崇箰椤潡鎳滃▓璺ㄥ姺闂佸憡蓱閻╊垶寮婚敓鐘茬劦妞ゆ帊鑳堕々鐑芥倵閿濆骸浜為柛姗€浜跺娲传閸曨偀鍋撴禒瀣垫晪鐟滃孩绌辨繝鍥х濞达絽婀遍崢?/li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}

function ThemeColorSetting({ label, presets, value, onChange }: { label: string; presets: string[]; value: string; onChange: (color: string) => void }) {
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgb(value);
  const isLight = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b) > 195;
  return (
    <section className="df-theme-setting">
      <div>
        <strong>{label}</strong>
        <span className={isLight ? "color-light" : ""} style={{ "--project-color": value } as CSSProperties} />
      </div>
      <ProjectColorPicker presets={presets} value={value} onChange={onChange} />
    </section>
  );
}

function SourceModal({ tasks, projects, today, onClose, onJoin }: { tasks: Task[]; projects: Project[]; today: string; onClose: () => void; onJoin: (taskIds: string[]) => void }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [showAdded, setShowAdded] = useState(false);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState("");
  const openTasks = tasks.filter((task) => {
    if (task.completed) return false;
    if (!showAdded && task.plannedForDate === today) return false;
    if (filter === "all") return true;
    if (filter === "unassigned") return !task.projectId;
    return task.projectId === filter;
  });
  const filteredProjects = projects.filter((project) => filter === "all" || filter === project.id);
  const selectedIds = Object.keys(selected).filter((id) => selected[id]);
  const toggleSelected = (taskId: string) => setSelected((current) => ({ ...current, [taskId]: !current[taskId] }));
  const addSelected = () => {
    onJoin(selectedIds);
    setSelected({});
    onClose();
  };
  return (
    <div className="df-modal" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="df-source" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="df-source-fixed">
          <div className="df-source-head">
            <h2>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪孩顐介柨鐔哄Т绾惧鏌涢弴銊ョ€柛銉墮缁犮儲銇勯弮鍌涘殌濞存粎鍋撻幈銊ノ熺粙鍨婵犵绱曢弲顐ゆ閹烘纾兼繛鎴烆焽閻熻尙绱撴担铏瑰笡缂佽鐗嗛悾鐑藉箳閹冲磭鍠栭幃鐑藉级閹稿海妲梻鍌氬€峰ù鍥х暦閻㈢绐楅柟閭﹀劒濞差亜绠ｉ柨鏃囨閳ь剙娼￠弻銊╁籍閸屾矮澹曢梺鍝勬缁捇骞冨畡鎵虫瀻闊洦鎼╂禒鐐箾鐎涙ê娈犻柛濠冪墱閹广垹鈽夐姀鐘殿吅闂佺粯鍔栭鏍汲閿熺姵鈷戝ù鍏肩懅閸掓壆鈧娲﹂崜鐔凤耿娓氣偓濮婅櫣绱掑Ο蹇氬亹閹峰啴鏁冮崒姘優濠电偛妫楃换鍡涘绩?/h2>
            <button className="df-icon-action i-close" data-tip="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? aria-label="闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佺懓顕崑鐔虹不閹€鏀介柣妯垮紦鏉? onClick={onClose} />
          </div>
          <div className="df-source-toolbar">
            <button className="light" onClick={() => setShowAdded((value) => !value)}>{showAdded ? "闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹閹间礁纾归柟闂寸缁犵娀鐓崶銊р槈缂佲偓婢跺绻嗘い鏍ㄧ懅缁夋寧绻涢幋鐐电叝婵炲矈浜炵槐鎺戔槈濮楀棗鍓伴梺鍝勬噽婵炩偓婵﹥妞藉畷銊︾節閸曨剙娅戞俊鐐€х€靛矂宕规导绮光偓锕傚炊椤掆偓缁狅綁鏌ㄩ弮鍌涙珪闁? : "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢妶鍌氫壕婵鍘ф晶顖炴煛閸涙澘鐓愮紒鍌涘笧閳ь剨缍嗛埀顒夊弿闂勫嫰骞堥妸銉建闁糕剝顨呴埛鎺楁⒑閸濄儱鏋庨柟铏耿瀵鈽夊Ο閿嬬€婚棅顐㈡祫缁插墽鐟у┑鐘垫暩閸嬫盯宕ョ€ｎ喖鍨傞柛褎顨呴拑?}</button>
            <button className="primary" disabled={selectedIds.length === 0} onClick={addSelected}>濠电姷鏁告慨鐑藉极閹间礁纾块柟瀵稿Х缁€濠囨煃瑜滈崜姘跺Φ閸曨垰鍗抽柛鈩冾殔椤忣亪鏌涘▎蹇曠闁哄矉缍侀獮鍥敊閻撳骸顬嗛梻浣虹帛鐢喖顢氳閳ワ妇鎹勯妸锕€纾繛鎾村嚬閸ㄨ鲸鐡忛梻鍌欑閹诧繝鏁冮埡鍛婵炲棙鎸撮埀?{selectedIds.length || ""}</button>
          </div>
          <div className="df-filter-row">
            <button className={filter === "all" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setFilter("all")}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸姀鈥冲簥闂佸壊鍋侀崕杈╃矆婢舵劖鐓欓弶鍫濆⒔閻ｈ京鐥幆褎鍋ラ柟顔筋殔閳藉鈻嶆潏銊х煁缂佸倸绉撮…銊╁醇閻斿搫骞堥梻浣瑰濡線顢氳閻涱喖螖閸愵亞锛?/button>
            <button className={filter === "unassigned" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setFilter("unassigned")}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/button>
            {projects.map((project) => <button key={project.id} className={filter === project.id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setFilter(project.id)}>{project.title}</button>)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="df-source-body">
          {openTasks.filter((task) => task.dueDate < today).length > 0 && <section className="df-source-section"><h3>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪孩顐介柨鐔哄Т绾惧鏌涢埄鍐槈缁炬儳顭烽弻鐔煎礈瑜滈崝婊堟煠閺夋寧鍋ラ柡灞界Ч閸┾剝鎷呴崨濠冾啀婵＄偑鍊愰弲婵嬪礂濮椻偓楠炲啳銇愰幒鎴犲€為梺闈涱焾閸庡磭绮婂畡閭︽富?/h3>
            {openTasks.filter((task) => task.dueDate < today).slice(0, 8).map((task) => <SourceRow key={task.id} task={task} today={today} projectName={projects.find((project) => project.id === task.projectId)?.title || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?} selected={Boolean(selected[task.id])} expanded={expanded === task.id} onSelect={() => toggleSelected(task.id)} onExpand={() => setExpanded((id) => id === task.id ? "" : task.id)} />)}
          </section>}
          {openTasks.filter((task) => task.dueDate >= today).length > 0 && <section className="df-source-section"><h3>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ら悧鍡欑矆閸喓绠鹃柛鈩兩戠亸顓犵磼閻橀潧鈻堥柡灞剧洴閸╁嫰宕橀崜褍濮烘俊鐐€愰弲婵嬪礂濮椻偓楠炲啳銇愰幒鎴犲€為梺闈涱焾閸庡磭绮婂畡閭︽富?/h3>
            {openTasks.filter((task) => task.dueDate >= today).slice(0, 8).map((task) => <SourceRow key={task.id} task={task} today={today} projectName={projects.find((project) => project.id === task.projectId)?.title || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?} selected={Boolean(selected[task.id])} expanded={expanded === task.id} onSelect={() => toggleSelected(task.id)} onExpand={() => setExpanded((id) => id === task.id ? "" : task.id)} />)}
          </section>}
          {filteredProjects.some((project) => openTasks.some((task) => task.projectId === project.id)) && <section className="df-source-section"><h3>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娴ｉ潻鑰块梺顒€绉埀顒婄畵瀹曞ジ濡风€ｎ亝鍠橀柟顔炬櫕缁瑧鎷犳穱鍗炲姢闁逞屽墮缁犲秹宕曢柆宓ュ洦瀵奸弶鎴狅紵闁瑰吋鎯屽鎸庣濠婂牊鐓涢柛鎰╁妼閳ь剛鎳撻埢宥夊即閵忥紕鍘梺鍓插亝缁诲秹鎮℃總鍛婄厵?/h3>
        {filteredProjects.map((project) => {
          const projectTasks = openTasks.filter((task) => task.projectId === project.id).slice(0, 8);
          if (projectTasks.length === 0) return null;
          return <section key={project.id}><strong>{project.title}</strong>{projectTasks.map((task) => <SourceRow key={task.id} task={task} today={today} projectName={project.title} selected={Boolean(selected[task.id])} expanded={expanded === task.id} onSelect={() => toggleSelected(task.id)} onExpand={() => setExpanded((id) => id === task.id ? "" : task.id)} />)}</section>;
        })}
          </section>}
          {filter === "unassigned" && openTasks.length > 0 && <section><strong>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?/strong>{openTasks.map((task) => <SourceRow key={task.id} task={task} today={today} projectName="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁? selected={Boolean(selected[task.id])} expanded={expanded === task.id} onSelect={() => toggleSelected(task.id)} onExpand={() => setExpanded((id) => id === task.id ? "" : task.id)} />)}</section>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SourceRow({ task, today, projectName, selected, expanded, onSelect, onExpand }: { task: Task; today: string; projectName: string; selected: boolean; expanded: boolean; onSelect: () => void; onExpand: () => void }) {
  const joined = task.plannedForDate === today;
  return (
    <div className={`df-source-row ${selected ? "selected" : ""} ${joined ? "joined" : ""}`}>
      <button className="df-source-select" onClick={(event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        onSelect();
      }}>{selected ? "闂? : "闂?}</button>
      <div className="df-source-main" onClick={onExpand}>
        <span>{task.title}</span>
        <small>{joined ? "闂傚倷娴囬褎顨ョ粙鍖¤€块梺顒€绉埀顒婄畵瀹曠厧鈹戦幇顒侇吙闂備礁澹婇崑鍛洪弽顓熺叆闁靛牆鎳夐弨浠嬫煟濡搫绾ч柟鍏煎姉缁辨帡鍩€? : `${projectName} 闂?${formatDuration(task.estimatedHours || 0.5)} 闂?闂傚倸鍊搁崐宄懊归崶顒婄稏濠㈣埖鍔曠粻姘舵倶閻愭彃鈷旀い鈺佸级缁绘繈妫冨☉鍗炲壘闂?${shortDate(task.dueDate)}`}</small>
        {expanded && <p>{task.notes || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢埗鑺ョ☉铻栭柛娑卞幘閿涙瑩姊洪懡銈呮瀾閻庢艾鐗嗛埥澶愬閻樻爠鍥ㄧ厱闁斥晛鍠氬▓妯肩磼閹邦喖浠辨慨濠冩そ楠炴牠鎮欓幓鎺戭潙闂備礁鎲￠弻銊х矓閻熼偊鍤?}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PlanningView(props: {
  data: PlannerData;
  projects: Project[];
  tasks: Task[];
  collapsed: Record<string, boolean>;
  setCollapsed: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Record<string, boolean>>>;
  pickMode: boolean;
  picks: Record<string, PlanPickPriority>;
  onExitPickMode: () => void;
  onAddPick: (taskId: string) => void;
  onUpdatePick: (taskId: string, priority: PlanPickPriority) => void;
  onRemovePick: (taskId: string) => void;
  onClearPicks: () => void;
  onApplyPicks: (scope: "today" | "week") => void;
  onProjectEdit: (project: Project) => void;
  onTaskEdit: (task: Task) => void;
  onTaskUpdate: (taskId: string, patch: Partial<Task>) => void;
  onTaskCreate: (projectId: string) => void;
  onTaskDelete: (taskId: string) => void;
}) {
  const unassigned = props.tasks.filter((task) => !task.projectId && !task.completed);
  const pickedTasks = Object.keys(props.picks)
    .map((id) => props.tasks.find((task) => task.id === id))
    .filter(Boolean) as Task[];
  const priorityGroups: Array<[PlanPickPriority, string]> = [["must", "闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佲€﹂鍕；闁告洦鍊嬪ú顏勎у璺猴功閻ゅ洤鈹戦濮愪粶闁稿鎸搁埞鎴︽晬閸曞灚顥撶紓浣哄У缁嬫垿鍩ユ径濞㈢喓娑垫搴¤€?], ["should", "闂傚倸鍊风粈浣革耿闁秴鍌ㄧ憸鏃堝箖濞差亜惟闁靛瀛╃粙鎺旂箔閻旂厧鐒垫い鎺戝閳ь兛绀侀～婵嬵敆婢跺鍏婇梺鑽ゅ█缂傛艾鈻嶉敐澶嬫櫖?], ["could", "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕闂佺硶鍓濋摂瀣炊椤掍胶鍔堕悗骞垮劚閹虫劙宕㈤幘顔解拺闁告繂瀚埢澶愭煕濡灝浜瑰?]];
  const projectName = (task: Task) => props.projects.find((project) => String(project.id) === String(task.projectId || ""))?.title || "闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁?;
  const addSubtask = (task: Task) => {
    const title = window.prompt("闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥敋瑜忛埀顒佺▓閺呮繄鍒掑▎鎾崇婵°倐鍋撶紒鈧径鎰厵闁绘垶锕╁▓鏃傜磼閻樺磭鍙€闁哄本娲濈粻娑氣偓锝庝簴閸嬫捇寮撮悩鐢电劶婵犮垼鍩栭崝鏍偂閸愵喗鐓忓璺虹墕婵′粙鏌ｅ┑鎰偗闁哄本绋掔换婵嬪磼濠婃劗绀婄紓?);
    if (!title?.trim()) return;
    props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, {
      subtasks: [...(task.subtasks || []), { id: uid("subtask"), title: title.trim(), completed: false, done: false, order: Date.now(), createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }]
    });
  };
  const setTaskDate = (task: Task) => {
    const date = window.prompt("闂傚倸鍊峰ù鍥х暦閸偅鍙忕€规洖娲ㄩ惌鍡椕归敐鍫綈婵炲懐濮撮湁闁绘ê妯婇崕鎰版煕鐎ｅ吀閭柡灞剧洴閸╁嫰宕橀浣诡潔缂傚倷绶￠崑鍕矓瑜版帒钃熼柡鍥╁枎缁剁偞绻涢幋娆忕仾妞ゅ骸绉瑰?YYYY-MM-DD", task.dueDate || todayIso());
    if (!date?.trim()) return;
    props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, { dueDate: date.trim() });
  };
  const moveTaskProject = (task: Task) => {
    const options = props.projects.map((project, index) => `${index + 1}. ${project.title}`).join("\n");
    const choice = window.prompt(`缂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪海鐭嗗〒姘ｅ亾閽樻繈鏌熼崜浣烘憘闁轰礁顑囬幉鍛婃償閵娿儳鐤勯梺闈浥堥弲娑㈡偂濞戙垺鐓曟繛鎴濆船楠炴ɑ銇勯敂璇蹭簼缂佺粯鐩鍫曞箣濠靛洤鈧垶姊烘导娆戠У濞存粠浜畷娲焵椤掍降浜滈柟鍝勬娴滃墽绱撴担鍓叉Ц缂傚秴锕悰顕€宕卞☉妯碱槹濡炪倖鐗徊楣冨疾椤忓牊鈷戠紒顖涙礀婢ф煡鏌ｉ悢婵嗘处閸嬫ɑ銇勮閺?. 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁绘稑鐣濇繝渚€鍋楅梺鍝勭焿缁查箖骞嗛弮鍫濈伋鐎规洖娲ょ敮?{options}`, "0");
    if (choice === null) return;
    const index = Number(choice) - 1;
    props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, { projectId: index >= 0 ? props.projects[index]?.id : undefined });
  };
  const renameTask = (task: Task) => {
    const title = window.prompt("缂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹閹间礁纾归柟闂寸绾惧湱鎲搁悧鍫濈瑲闁稿顑夐弻锝夊箛椤掑倷绮靛銈嗗姌婵倝濡甸崟顖氱疀闁告挷鑳堕弳鐘绘煟閻斿摜鎳曞┑鈥虫喘閸┾偓妞ゆ巻鍋撶紒鐘茬Ч瀹曟洟宕￠悙鈺傜亙?, task.title);
    if (!title?.trim()) return;
    props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, { title: title.trim() });
  };
  return (
    <main className={`df-planning ${props.pickMode ? "pick-mode" : ""}`}>
      <section className="df-mindmap no-root">
        {props.pickMode && <div className="df-pick-banner"><strong>濠电姷鏁告慨鐢割敊閺嶎厼绐楁俊銈呭暞瀹曟煡鏌熼柇锕€鏋涚紒韬插€濋弻锕€螣娓氼垱顎嗛梺鑲╁鐎笛囧Φ閸曨喚鐤€闁圭偓娼欏▍褍顪冮妶鍌涙珖闁稿繑锕㈤悰顕€寮介‖銉ラ叄椤㈡鍩€椤掑嫬鐒垫い鎴炲劤閳ь剚娲熼垾锕傚炊椤掆偓缁犳盯鏌ｅΔ鈧悧蹇涘储闁秵鈷戦柛锔诲幖閸斿鏌熺粙鎸庢喐缂侇噮鍙冨畷鎺楁倷鐎电骞橀梻浣稿閻撳牓宕戦崟顖涘€堕柟缁㈠枟閻撴盯鎮橀悙璺盒撻柛锝堟閳ь剙鐏氬妯尖偓姘煎櫍閸┾偓妞ゆ帒锕︾粔闈浢瑰鍡楃厫缂佸倹甯為埀顒佺⊕椤洨绮绘ィ鍐╁€甸柣銏☆問閻掗箖妫勭€ｎ亶娓婚柕鍫濇閼茬娀鏌涢弴銊ュ鐎?/strong><span>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸撲礁鍨濇い鏍仜缁€澶嬩繆閵堝懏鍣圭紒鐘靛█閺岀喖骞戦幇闈涙闂佸憡淇洪～澶愬Φ閸曨垰绫嶉柛灞剧⊕閻濐亜顪冮妶鍌涙珖闁稿繑锕㈤獮鍐亹閹烘垹鍊為梺闈涱焾閸庡磭绮婂畡閭︽富闁靛牆楠告禍鏍煕婵犲啰绠炵€殿喖顭烽弫鎾绘偐閼碱剦妲伴柣鐔哥矋閸ㄥ灝鐣峰Δ鍐ф勃闁告挆鍜佸晭?+ 闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鍨鹃幇浣圭稁缂傚倷鐒﹁摫闁告瑥绻橀弻鐔虹磼閵忕姵鐏堥梺娲诲幗椤ㄥ﹪寮诲☉銏犵労闁告劦浜栧Σ鍫㈢磼閻愵剙绀冩い顐㈩槺閹广垹鈽夐姀鐘茶€垮┑鈽嗗灥椤曆囨儍閹达附鈷戠紒瀣儥閸庢劙鏌熷宀婂殭妞ゆ洏鍎靛畷鐔碱敇濞戞ü澹曢梺鎸庣箓閹虫劖绂掑☉銏＄厸濞撴埃鍋撳ù婊嗘硾椤繐煤椤忓嫮顔囬柟鑹版彧缁插搫危閸儲鈷戞繛鑼额嚙楠炴牠鏌涙繝鍌涘仴鐎规洘妞介崺鈧い鎺嶉檷娴滄粓鏌熼崫鍕棞濞存粍鍎抽—鍐Χ韫囨挾妲ｉ梺鎼炲姀閸╂牗绌辨繝鍥ㄥ殝闁汇垺顔栧ù鍕煟鎼搭垳绉靛ù婊愮秬椤ゅ倿姊绘担渚敯闁稿鍔楅幃浼村Ψ閳轰胶鍔﹀銈嗗笂缁€浣规櫠椤栨稓绠惧┑鍌炴櫜閹茬偓鎱ㄦ繝鍛仩缂侇喗鐟ч幑鍕Ω瑜忛弶浠嬫⒒娴ｄ警鐒鹃悗娑掓櫆缁旂喖宕卞☉妯碱槴闂佸湱鍎ら〃鍛村礃閳ь剙顪冮妶鍡樺暗闁稿鍠栭幆鍫ュ礋椤栨稈鎷洪梻鍌氱墛娓氭顬婅缁辨帡鎮╅崘鑼紝闂佺硶鏅滈惄顖氼嚕椤曗偓瀹曞ジ鎮㈤摎鍌氭憢濠电姷鏁搁崑鐐哄垂閸洍鈧箓宕奸妷顖滅◤闂佹寧娲栭崐褰掓偂?/span><button onClick={props.onExitPickMode}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪孩顐介柨鐔哄Т绾惧鏌嶈閸撶喖寮诲☉姘ｅ亾閿濆骸浜濈€规洖鐭傞弻?/button></div>}
        <div className="df-tree">
          {props.projects.map((project) => {
            const projectTasks = props.tasks.filter((task) => String(task.projectId || "") === String(project.id) && !task.completed);
            return (
              <div className="df-category-branch" key={project.id}>
                <button className="df-collapse" onClick={() => props.setCollapsed((current) => ({ ...current, [project.id]: !current[project.id] }))}>{props.collapsed[project.id] ? "+" : "-"}</button>
                <PlanningProjectNode title={project.title} onOpen={() => props.onProjectEdit(project)} onAddTask={() => props.onTaskCreate(project.id)} />
                {!props.collapsed[project.id] && <div className="df-project-list"><div className="df-task-branch">{projectTasks.map((task) => (
                  <PlanningTaskNode key={task.id} task={task} projectName={project.title} picked={Boolean(props.picks[task.id])} onOpen={() => props.onTaskEdit(task)} onAdd={() => props.onAddPick(task.id)} onStar={() => props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, { priority: "high", importance: "high" })} onRename={() => renameTask(task)} onAddSubtask={() => addSubtask(task)} onSetDate={() => setTaskDate(task)} onMoveProject={() => moveTaskProject(task)} onDelete={() => props.onTaskDelete(task.id)} />
                ))}</div></div>}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {unassigned.length > 0 && (
            <div className="df-category-branch">
              <button className="df-collapse" onClick={() => props.setCollapsed((current) => ({ ...current, unassigned: !current.unassigned }))}>{props.collapsed.unassigned ? "+" : "-"}</button>
              <PlanningProjectNode title="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁绘稑鐣濇繝渚€鍋楅梺鍝勭焿缂嶄線鐛崶顒夋晩缁炬媽椴稿В澶愭煟鎼淬値娼愭繛鍙夛耿閹繝鏁撻悩鑼舵憰? onAddTask={() => props.onTaskCreate("")} />
              {!props.collapsed.unassigned && <div className="df-project-list"><div className="df-task-branch">{unassigned.map((task) => (
                <PlanningTaskNode key={task.id} task={task} projectName="闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕濠电姴锕ょ€氼噣銆呴崣澶岀瘈濠电姴鍊归崳鐣岀磼閳ь剚绻濋崒妤佹杸闂佺粯蓱瑜板啴顢旇缁? picked={Boolean(props.picks[task.id])} onOpen={() => props.onTaskEdit(task)} onAdd={() => props.onAddPick(task.id)} onStar={() => props.onTaskUpdate(task.id, { priority: "high", importance: "high" })} onRename={() => renameTask(task)} onAddSubtask={() => addSubtask(task)} onSetDate={() => setTaskDate(task)} onMoveProject={() => moveTaskProject(task)} onDelete={() => props.onTaskDelete(task.id)} />
              ))}</div></div>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="df-pick-panel">
        <div className="df-pick-panel-head"><strong>闂傚倸鍊搁崐鐑芥嚄閸洍鈧箓宕奸妷锔芥珖闂侀潧顦弲婊堝磻閿熺姵鐓冮柛婵嗗閸ｅ綊鏌ｉ幒鎴犱粵闁靛洤瀚伴獮鎺楀幢濡炴儳顥氬┑锛勫亼閸娧呪偓闈涚焸瀹曟娊鏁愭径灞界ウ闂佺鎻粻鎴︼綖閸涘瓨鐓忛柛顐ｇ箖閸ｈ姤銇?/strong><span>{pickedTasks.length} 婵?/span></div>
        {pickedTasks.length === 0 ? <div className="df-pick-empty">婵犵數濮烽弫鎼佸磻濞戙埄鏁嬫い鎾跺枑閸欏繘鎮楀☉娆欎緵婵炲牅绮欓弻娑樷槈濮楀牆濮涢梺宕囩帛濞茬喖鎮￠锕€鐐婇柕濠忚吂閹峰姊洪懡銈呮瀻婵犫偓闁秴绠熼柟闂寸缁秹鏌涢锝嗗剷闁靛／鈧崑鎾荤嵁閸喖濮庡銈忕細閸楀啿鐣烽弴鐔稿劅妞ゎ偒鍏涚花濠氭⒑閸濆嫬鏆欓柛濠勬嚀閳诲秹寮撮姀锛勫幈闂佸啿鎼崐鍛婃叏閸ヮ剚鐓冮柦妯侯樈濡偓濡ょ姷鍋為悷鈺佺暦濮椻偓婵℃悂濡疯濞煎姊婚崒娆戠獢闁逞屽墰閸嬫盯鎳熼娑欐珷妞ゆ柨顫曟禍婊堟煥閺冨洦顥夋い銉ヮ儔閺屽秷顧侀柛鎾村哺瀹曨偅鎯旈妸銉ь槷閻庡箍鍎卞ú銊╂儗婢舵劖鐓欓柣鎴炆戠亸鐢电磼閳锯偓閸嬫捇姊绘担渚劸闁哄牜鍓涘﹢渚€姊洪崨濠傜仯缂侇喗鐟ラ～蹇涙惞鐟欏嫬鐝伴梺鐐藉劚绾绢厽绂掗弶鎴旀斀闁绘劕寮堕崳鐑樼箾閸欏鐭掔€殿噮鍋婇、娆撴倷椤掆偓閺嬪倿姊洪崨濠冨闁稿繑绋戦～婵嬫晝閳ь剟鍩為幋锔藉€烽柡澶嬪灩娴犲摜绱掗悙顒佺凡缂佸缍婇獮鍐┿偅閸愩劎顢呴梺璇茬箺椤曆呯礊婵犲洤绠栭柍鍝勫€婚悷瑙勩亜閺嶃劎顣叉い鏂垮閳?/div> : priorityGroups.map(([priority, label]) => {
          const groupTasks = pickedTasks.filter((task) => props.picks[task.id] === priority);
          return <div className="df-pick-group" key={priority}><h3>{label}</h3>{groupTasks.length === 0 ? <small>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢埗鑺ョ☉铻栭柛娑卞幘閿涙瑩姊洪懡銈呮瀾閻?/small> : groupTasks.map((task) => (
            <article key={task.id} className="df-pick-card">
              <div><strong>{task.title}</strong><span># {projectName(task)}</span></div>
              <select value={props.picks[task.id]} onChange={(event) => props.onUpdatePick(task.id, event.target.value as PlanPickPriority)}>
                <option value="must">闂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佲€﹂鍕；闁告洦鍊嬪ú顏勎у璺猴功閻ゅ洤鈹戦濮愪粶闁稿鎸搁埞鎴︽晬閸曞灚顥撶紓浣哄У缁嬫垿鍩ユ径濞㈢喓娑垫搴¤€?/option>
                <option value="should">闂傚倸鍊风粈浣革耿闁秴鍌ㄧ憸鏃堝箖濞差亜惟闁靛瀛╃粙鎺旂箔閻旂厧鐒垫い鎺戝閳ь兛绀侀～婵嬵敆婢跺鍏婇梺鑽ゅ█缂傛艾鈻嶉敐澶嬫櫖?/option>
                <option value="could">闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炴牠顢曢敂钘変罕闂佺硶鍓濋摂瀣炊椤掍胶鍔堕悗骞垮劚閹虫劙宕㈤幘顔解拺闁告繂瀚埢澶愭煕濡灝浜瑰?/option>
              </select>
              <button onClick={() => props.onRemovePick(task.id)}>缂傚倸鍊搁崐鎼佸磹妞嬪海鐭嗗〒姘ｅ亾閽樻繃銇勯弽顐汗闁逞屽墾缁犳垿鎮鹃敓鐘茬闁惧浚鍋嗛埀?/button>
            </article>
          ))}</div>;
        })}
        <div className="df-pick-actions">
          <button className="primary" disabled={pickedTasks.length === 0} onClick={() => props.onApplyPicks("today")}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鍨鹃幇浣圭稁缂傚倷鐒﹁摫闁告瑥绻橀弻鐔虹磼閵忕姵鐏堥梺娲诲幗椤ㄥ﹪寮诲☉銏犵労闁告劕澧介崝宄邦渻閵堝倹娅呴柛銏＄叀閸╃偤骞嬮敂钘変汗闂佸湱绮敮妤€鈻撻弻銉︹拺闁圭娴烽埥澶嬨亜閿旂偓鏆€殿喖顭烽幃銏ゆ惞閸︻厾鏆ユ繝鐢靛仦閸ㄦ儼鎽梺鎸庣☉缁夊綊寮婚敓鐘茬劦?/button>
          <button disabled={pickedTasks.length === 0} onClick={() => props.onApplyPicks("week")}>闂傚倸鍊搁崐椋庣矆娓氣偓楠炲鍨鹃幇浣圭稁缂傚倷鐒﹁摫闁告瑥绻橀弻鐔虹磼閵忕姵鐏堥梺娲诲幗椤ㄥ﹪寮诲☉銏犵労闁告劦浜栧Σ鍫ユ煟鎼淬垼澹樻い锔垮嵆婵＄敻宕熼姘鳖唺闂佺硶鍓濋妵鐐寸珶閺囥垺鈷掑ù锝囧劋閸も偓闂佸摜鍠庨悘婵婄亱濠电偞鍨崹鍦不椤栫偞鍊甸柨婵嗛閺嬫盯鏌ｈ箛鏂库枙闁哄本鐩獮鍥敍濮樿鲸顕楀┑?/button>
          <button className="light" disabled={pickedTasks.length === 0} onClick={props.onClearPicks}>濠电姷鏁告慨鐑藉极閹间礁纾婚柣鎰惈缁犱即鏌熼梻瀵割槮缂佺姷濞€閺岀喖鎮ч崼鐔哄嚒闂佺粯鎸婚敃銏ゅ蓟閳ユ剚鍚嬮幖绮光偓宕囶啇缂備胶鍋撳畷姗€锝炴径灞惧床婵炴垯鍨归獮銏′繆椤栨繍鍤欓柣銊﹀灴濮?/button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

function PlanningTaskNode(props: {
  task: Task;
  projectName: string;
  picked: boolean;
  onOpen: () => void;
  onAdd: () => void;
  onRename: () => void;
  onAddSubtask: () => void;
  onSetDate: () => void;
  onMoveProject: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}) {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className={`df-plan-task-node ${props.picked ? "picked" : ""}`}> 
      <button className="df-task-node" onClick={props.onOpen}>
        <span>{props.task.title}</span>
        {(props.task.subtasks || []).length > 0 && <small>{props.task.subtasks?.filter((sub) => sub.completed || sub.done).length}/{props.task.subtasks?.length}</small>}
        {props.picked && <em>Picked</em>}
      </button>
      <div className="df-plan-node-actions">
        <button title="Join candidate" onClick={props.onAdd}>&rarr;</button>
        <button title="More" onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}>&hellip;</button>
      </div>
      {menuOpen && <div className="df-plan-more">
        <button onClick={() => { props.onRename(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>Rename</button>
        <button onClick={() => { props.onAddSubtask(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>Add subtask</button>
        <button onClick={() => { props.onSetDate(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>Set date</button>
        <button onClick={() => { props.onMoveProject(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>Move to project</button>
        <button className="danger" onClick={() => { props.onDelete(); setMenuOpen(false); }}>Delete</button>
      </div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function PlanningProjectNode(props: { title: string; onOpen?: () => void; onAddTask: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="df-plan-project-node">
      <button className="df-category-node project-root" onClick={props.onOpen}>{props.title}</button>
      <button className="df-plan-project-add" title="濠电姷鏁告慨鐑藉极閹间礁纾块柟瀵稿Х缁€濠囨煃瑜滈崜姘跺Φ閸曨垰鍗抽柛鈩冾殔椤忣亪鏌涘▎蹇曠闁哄矉缍侀獮鍥敆婢跺﹥顔嶆俊鐐€愰弲婵嬪礂濮椻偓楠炲啳銇愰幒鎴犲€為梺闈涱焾閸庡磭绮婂畡閭︽富? onClick={props.onAddTask}>+</button>
    </div>
  );
}

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root")!;
const rootKey = "__plannerRoot";
const rootWindow = window as typeof window & { [rootKey]?: ReturnType<typeof createRoot> };
const root = rootWindow[rootKey] ?? createRoot(rootElement);
rootWindow[rootKey] = root;
root.render(<App />);
